# W1 施工圖：Back 核心

> 狀態：`DOC_READY`（本檔合併即是）。上游：[01 §12](../01-frontend-sdd.md#12-實作波次給-llm-agent) W1 列、[README § 路線圖](../README.md#路線圖)。
>
> 讀法：本檔是**唯一**需要照做的文件。§5 的程式全文都在預演環境中通過 §9 的完整閘門（2026-09-25）；實作者**逐字**建立或取代檔案，不要自行改寫、重新命名或「順手整理」。§6 的任務卡決定順序；每張卡的「驗證」指令轉綠才進下一張。
>
> 本波的起點是 **W0 已實作完成**的程式（[W0.md](W0.md) §5 的結果）。§5 列為「取代」的檔案，以本檔全文整個覆蓋；列為「修改」的檔案，只改 §5 寫出的片段。

### 1.2 不做

- 01 §1.2 的非目標；AGENTS.md「不要做」。
- **W2 的範圍**：`MediaPicker`、`RelationPicker`（`ref`、`media-ref` 在 W1 **唯讀**顯示，新條目無法設定它們）、`/media`、預覽、修訂紀錄、三個自訂視圖的行為重做（C-08、C-09、C-10、U-03）。W1 只改自訂視圖的**文案與標籤**（U-01、U-04、G-06）。
- 「請求發布」按鈕（G-03，BW2）。
- 日期範圍篩選的 UI（`filter.<field>.from/.to`）：API 與 `@cms/api` 已支援，但 index 只做 enum 篩選；行程視圖在 W2 使用範圍篩選。
- `principal-ref` 的選擇器（G-04，BW2）：唯讀顯示「已連結會員帳號」。
- Admin、Front 的任何畫面（W3、W4）。`apps/web-front`、`apps/web-admin` 本波**不改**。
- 01 §10.2 的 bundle 預算（W5，見 §4.10）。
- 帳號選單的「查看公開網站 ↗」（B-S1）：需要每個類型的公開首頁對照，併入 W3 之後的 W5 硬化；W1 帳號選單維持 W0 的顯示名＋角色＋登出。
- 側欄的「⌘K 跳到類型或條目」（B-S1 線框中的搜尋框）：沒有對應的 API，本波不做，由 W5 評估。

## 2. 先決條件

### 2.1 前置波次

| 波次 | 需要的狀態 | 依賴它的哪些產出 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| W0 | `VERIFIED` | 全部 packages、三個 app 的新殼、MSW、`e2e:mock`、copy 規則、`test:bundle`。W1 的「修改」全部以 W0 的結果為基準 |
| BW1a | `DOC_READY` | `Me.capabilities`、`WorkContentType` 的類型設定、`WorkField` 的中繼資料（[BW1a.md](BW1a.md) §4、§5.3） |
| BW1b | `DOC_READY` | 列表查詢文法、`total`（[BW1b.md](BW1b.md) §4.3） |
| BW1c | `DOC_READY` | `error.fields`、`FieldErrorCode`、428 `VERSION_REQUIRED`、`EntryPatchRequest`（[BW1c.md](BW1c.md) §4） |

**不需要後端已實作。** 依 [01 Q-10](../01-frontend-sdd.md#133-w0-細化時新增已決定owner2026-09-25)，前端依自己的契約開發：W1 的 codegen 讀 [`contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml`](../contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml)（BW0→BW1a→BW1b→BW1c 累積的完整契約，`info.version` 0.7.0），**不讀** `services/cms-api/src/main/resources/openapi/openapi.yaml`。整合階段再把 `gen` 指回 `openapi.yaml`（§4.2）。

**防呆（T01 第 1 步）：** 開工時執行

```bash
cd apps/cms-scaffold
python3 -c "import yaml;d=yaml.safe_load(open('docs/v2/contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml'));print(d['info']['version'])"
```

輸出必須是 `0.7.0`。不是就停下來回報（契約已改版，本檔的型別與 fixture 需要重新細化）。

### 2.2 環境

與 [W0.md §2.2](W0.md#22-環境) 相同：Node 22（CI 用 `actions/setup-node` 的 22）、npm 10、不需要 JDK、Docker 或後端。所有指令在 `apps/cms-scaffold/` 下執行。Playwright 的瀏覽器：CI 由 workflow 安裝；本機已有 Chromium 時設 `PLAYWRIGHT_CHROMIUM_EXECUTABLE=<路徑>`（W0 §5.9）。本波不新增環境變數到 CI；`VITE_FRONT_ORIGIN` 放在 `apps/web-back/.env`（§5.7.1），沒有秘密。

## 4. 契約

### 4.1 API

本波**不新增** API。用到的 operation 全部在 [`contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml`](../contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml)。下表是 web-back 對每個 operation 的呼叫點與錯誤處理，實作者照做，不要另加處理。

| operation | 呼叫者（§5） | 會處理的錯誤 → 畫面 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET /api/v1/auth/me` | `SessionProvider`（W0） | 401 → 未登入（W0） |
| `GET /api/v1/content-types` | `Frame`（側欄標籤）、`HomePage` | 任何錯誤：側欄只顯示「首頁」與視圖；Home 顯示 `ErrorState` |
| `GET /api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}` | `TypeGate` | 404 → `/not-found`；403 → `/forbidden`；其他 → `ErrorState` |
| `GET /api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}/entries` | `IndexBody`、三個視圖 | 任何錯誤 → `ErrorState`（重試）。400 不會發生：URL 參數先經 `readIndexState` 正規化（W1-FM04） |
| `GET /api/v1/entries/{id}` | `EntryLoader`、衝突 dialog 的「載入最新版本」、`RefValue` | 404 → `/not-found`；403 → `/forbidden`；其他 → `ErrorState` |
| `POST /api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}/entries` | `EntryEditor`（新條目） | 422 → 欄位錯誤＋toast `details.fixFields`；409 `SLUG_CONFLICT` → 網址代稱錯誤；409 `SINGLETON_EXISTS` → toast `details.singletonExists`；其他 → toast `details.failed` |
| `PATCH /api/v1/entries/{id}` | `EntryEditor`（儲存）、三個視圖 | 422 → 欄位錯誤；409 `VERSION_CONFLICT` → 衝突 dialog；409 `SLUG_CONFLICT` → 網址代稱錯誤；409 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` → toast `details.stateChanged` 並重新載入；其他 → toast `details.failed`。視圖：任何錯誤 → toast `view.failed` |
| `POST /api/v1/entries/{id}/publish` | `EntryEditor` | 422 `SLUG_REQUIRED` → 網址代稱「必填」；422 其他 → 欄位錯誤；409 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` → 同上；其他 → toast |
| `POST …/unpublish`、`…/archive`、`…/restore` | `EntryEditor` | 409 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` → 同上；其他 → toast |
| `DELETE /api/v1/entries/{id}` | `EntryEditor` | 409 `REF_CONSTRAINT` → toast `details.referenced`；其他 → toast |
| `GET /api/v1/media/{id}` | `MediaValue` | 任何錯誤 → 「無法顯示這個媒體」 |

401 一律由 `@cms/auth` 處理：已登入時收到 401，session 變成 `expired`，`RequireSurface` **保留目前頁面**並經由路由導到 `/sign-in?returnTo=<目前路徑>`；有未存變更時，`ContextualSaveBar` 的 blocker 先問「離開這個頁面？」（01 §8、W1-FM09）。W0 的做法（把 `me` 設為 `null`）會先卸載頁面，未存的輸入直接消失，所以本波修改（§5.5）。

**成功與失敗範例**（MSW 與 BW1c 契約一致，§7 的測試逐一斷言）：

```http
PATCH /api/v1/entries/30000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000035
X-CSRF-Token: mock-csrf-token
Content-Type: application/json

{"version":1,"payload":{"title":"Buy more film","priority":null}}

HTTP/1.1 200 OK
{"id":"30000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000035","contentType":"note","slug":"buy-film","publicationState":"draft","version":2,"title":"Buy more film", …}
```

```http
POST /api/v1/entries/30000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000035/publish

HTTP/1.1 422 Unprocessable Entity
{"error":{"code":"REF_TARGET_NOT_FOUND","message":"1 invalid field(s); first: …",
  "fields":[{"field":"payload.related","code":"REF_TARGET_NOT_FOUND","message":"…"}]},"requestId":"mock-422-1"}
```

### 4.2 型別與 codegen

- `packages/api/package.json` 的 `gen`：`openapi-typescript ../../docs/v2/contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml -o src/generated/schema.d.ts`。
- `codegen.test.ts` 比對的來源同步改成同一個檔（§5.1）。
- **整合階段**（不在本波）：把兩處路徑改回 `../../services/cms-api/src/main/resources/openapi/openapi.yaml`，重新 `npm run gen`，修正型別差異。01 Q-10 已記錄。
- 新的型別別名（`packages/api/src/schema.ts`）：`EntryPatchRequest`、`FieldError`、`FieldErrorCode`、`Capabilities`、`TypeCapability`。畫面程式只從 `@cms/api` 取型別，不手寫 API 形狀（01 D-04）。

### 4.3 Query key

沿用 W0 的 `keys` factory（01 §4.3），W1 的變化：

| key | 形狀 | 用途 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `keys.entries.list(type, params)` | `["entries","list",type,WorkListParams]` | `params` 含 `state`、`q`、`filter`、`ref`、`sort`、`page`、`size`；物件內容不同就是不同的 key |
| `keys.entries.lists(type)` | `["entries","list",type]` | 儲存、發布、刪除後 `invalidateQueries` 的前綴 |
| `keys.entries.detail(id)` | `["entries","detail",id]` | 儲存或轉換狀態成功後以 `setQueryData` 寫入回應 |
| `keys.media.detail(id)` | `["media","detail",id]` | `MediaValue` |
| `keys.types.list()`、`keys.types.detail(type)` | 不變 | 側欄、Home、`TypeGate` |

列表用 `placeholderData: keepPreviousData`：換頁、換 tab 時舊資料留在畫面上直到新資料到達（不閃 skeleton）。

### 4.4 URL 狀態（Resource index）

01 §4.4：index 的所有設定都在 URL。

| 參數 | 值 | 預設（預設值不寫進 URL） | 對應 API 參數 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `state` | `draft`／`published`／`archived` | 省略＝tab「全部」 | 省略＝API 預設 `draft,published`；其他值原樣送 |
| `q` | 任意字串（前後空白去掉） | 空 | `q` |
| `sort` | `-updatedAt`／`updatedAt`／`title`／`-title`／`-createdAt` | `-updatedAt` | `sort`（一律送出） |
| `page` | 正整數 | 1 | `page`（一律送出） |
| `size` | `20`／`50`／`100` | 20 | `size`（一律送出） |
| `filter.<key>` | 該欄位的 `enumValues` 之一 | 無 | `filter.<key>` |

正規化規則（`readIndexState`，§5.7）：不在上表的值一律改用預設；`filter.<key>` 只接受 `filterable` 且 `type` 為 `enum`、`enumValues` 非空的欄位，其值必須在 `enumValues` 內，否則丟棄。改 tab、搜尋、篩選、排序、每頁筆數時，`page` 回到 1（從 URL 刪除）。

### 4.5 MSW fixture（`contracts/fixtures/`，本 PR 已更新）

W1 的 fixture 是 BW1c 契約的實例；`npm run typecheck` 透過 `fixtures.gen.ts` 的型別註記檢查。與 W0 版本的差異：

| 檔案 | 變化 |
| --- | --- |
| `capabilities.json` | **新增**。`{ <username>: { front: Capabilities, back: Capabilities, admin: Capabilities } }`，依 [BW1a.md](BW1a.md) §4.4 的規則由角色計算（member 的 `owner`、`pet`、`visit` 為 `scoped: true`；非 admin 的 `admin` surface 為空） |
| `me.json` | 加 `mock-operator-notes`（operator，`contentTypeCodes: ["note"]`）；每筆**不含** `capabilities`（mock 依目前 surface 從 `capabilities.json` 補上） |
| `principals.json` | 加 `mock-operator-notes`（共 10 筆） |
| `work-content-types.json`、`admin-content-types.json` | 類型加 `sortField`、`visibilityField`、`ownerField`、`singleton`、`previewable`；欄位加 `label`、`helpText`、`group`、`order`、`listable`、`filterable`、`enumLabels`、`placeholder`、`visibility`（BW1a §5.3 的種子值）；新增 demo 以外的 `note` 類型（含未知型別 `geo` 的 `location` 欄） |
| `work-entries.json` | 新增 3 筆 `note`（`buy-film` draft、`lens-notes` published、`spring-ideas` published＋`dirty`、version 2）；version 規則：建立＝1，發布／下架／封存／還原不變，PATCH＋1（BW1c） |
| 其餘 4 檔 | 不變 |

`packages/mocks/fixtures/` 的 10 個檔案與 `docs/v2/contracts/fixtures/` **位元組相同**（`cmp` 檢查，T03）。

### 4.6 文案

W0 §4.6 的 copy 規則不變（key 命名、只放在 `copy.ts`、自動檢查）。W1 新增 `@cms/fields` 的 `fieldsCopy`（§5.3）；web-back 的 `copy.ts` 整個取代（§5.6）；`@cms/ui` 的 `uiCopy` 取代（§5.2）。完整的 `key | zh-Hant | 出現位置` 表在 §5.6。

**工程詞禁用清單**（V2-AC-15）：可見文字不得含 `PATCH`、`sortOrder`、`origin`、`(string)`。`views.test.tsx` 檢查每一條 copy 與 6 個畫面的 `textContent`；`back-w1.spec.ts` 檢查 5 個畫面的 `innerText`。

### 4.7 權限：一律取自 `me.capabilities`

| 判斷 | 規則 | 程式 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 類型可作業（側欄、Home） | 該類型的 `actions` 含 `read_draft`、`create`、`update` 任一 | `workTypeKeys` |
| 能進 index／details | `read_draft` | `TypeGate` |
| 「新增」按鈕、`/new` | `create`（單例類型已有一筆時隱藏按鈕） | `IndexBody`、`NewEntryPage` |
| 可編輯 | `update`，且狀態不是 `archived` | `EntryEditor` 的 `readOnly` |
| 發布、發布變更 | `publish` | `EntryEditor` 的 `primary` |
| 下架 | `unpublish` | 發布卡 |
| 封存、還原為草稿 | `archive` | `moreActions` |
| 移到回收 | `delete` | `moreActions` |
| 自訂視圖 | 讀取的每個類型都可作業（`album.composer`：album＋photo；`clinic.schedule`：visit；`projects.board`：project＋issue） | `viewsFor` |

`scoped: true`（BW1a）表示部分條目仍會 403：details 收到 403 就導到 `/forbidden`。W1 的 Back 使用者沒有 scoped 能力（只有 member 有，而 member 進不了 Back）。

| `react-hook-form` | 7.88.0 | 新增 | `@cms/fields`、`apps/web-back` `dependencies` | 01 D-05 |
| `zod` | 4.6.5 | 新增 | `@cms/fields` | 01 D-05 |

### 4.9 shadcn 元件（第 3 批）

方法同 [W0.md §4.9](W0.md#49-shadcnui-初始化與元件清單)：上游 new-york-v4 原始碼，`import { cn } from "cn"` 換成 `import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"`；上游以 `@/registry/new-york-v4/ui/button` 引用 Button 的，換成 `./button`。**唯一的樣式修改**是 `tabs.tsx` 的觸發器文字色（下表註 1）。

| 元件 | 上游行數 | 上游 SHA-256（前 16 碼） | 相對上游的修改 | 用在哪裡 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `table` | 115 | `bd1a9fc83a5a2149` | `cn` import | `IndexTable` |
| `tabs` | 90 | `e1df6e2f95ece373` | `cn` import；註 1 | `IndexFilters`、Markdown 編輯／預覽 |
| `select` | 189 | `962830436bd0b7ee` | `cn` import | `IndexFilters`、`IndexPagination`、enum（> 5 個選項） |
| `radio-group` | 44 | `cfc4f99ee68be2fb` | `cn` import | enum（≤ 5 個選項） |
| `switch` | 34 | `743676f33f823ffa` | `cn` import | boolean |
| `dialog` | 158 | `2ade6074e5fad0a4` | `cn` import；Button import | 衝突 dialog |
| `alert-dialog` | 196 | `b3dfb83e54ed8c82` | `cn` import；Button import | 離開確認、封存、移到回收 |
| `popover` | 88 | `3f99d3add6c9fa4f` | `cn` import | datetime 日期選擇 |
| `calendar` | 220 | `78ed2cc49dafc5bb` | `cn` import；Button import | datetime 日期選擇 |

註 1：上游觸發器用 `text-foreground/60`，在 `bg-muted`（`--surface-subdued` #F7F7F7）上 axe 判定對比不足（V2-AC-14）。改為 `text-muted-foreground`（`--text-subdued` #616161，對 #F7F7F7 為 5.48:1，對 hover 底 #EBEBEB 為 5.20:1）。

`Sonner` 不用上游的 `sonner.tsx`（它依賴 `next-themes`，不在 SDD）；`@cms/ui` 的 `Toaster`（§5.2）直接包 `sonner` 的 `Toaster`，顏色取 tokens。`Form`（shadcn 的 react-hook-form 包裝）不採用：`FieldWidget` 直接用 `Controller`，錯誤文字與 `aria-describedby` 自己接（§5.3），少一層 context。

### 4.10 bundle 大小（W5 的輸入，不是本波閘門）

預演的 web-back 首頁 JS（`dist/assets/index-*.js`，以 `gzip -c <檔> | wc -c` 量測）：174.8KB；W0 為 162.8KB（[W0.md §4.10](W0.md#410-w0-的-bundle-大小給-w5-的基準不是本波閘門)）。增加的部分是 `Toaster`（`sonner`）與 `FieldsProvider` 進入首頁 chunk。畫面在 lazy chunk：`resource-details-*.js` 105.8KB（含 `react-markdown`、`zod`、`react-hook-form`、`react-day-picker`）、`type-gate-*.js` 9.8KB、`resource-index-*.js` 3.7KB。01 §10.2 的 160KB 預算由 W5 處理。

---
## 5. 模組與元件規格

§5.0 是 W1 必寫的**畫面規格**（線框、資料來源、狀態、互動）；§5.1 起是每個套件的元件規格與程式全文。畫面引用的元件，其 props、狀態、`data-testid`、copy key 都在 §5.2、§5.3、§5.7 的元件規格表，程式全文是權威。

tokens 一律用 W0 §4.4 的名稱（`text-subdued`、`text-critical`、`bg-surface`、`bg-surface-subdued`、`text-page-title`、`text-card-title`、`text-table`、`bg-primary`、`text-primary-foreground`、`bg-success-bg`、`text-success`、`bg-caution-bg`、`text-caution`、`text-link`）。本波沒有新增 token。

### 5.0 畫面

#### 5.0.1 B-S1 殼層（`BackShell`、`Frame`）

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ◼ CMS 作業台                                                (帳號) ▾    │ 頂列（W0 AppFrame）
├───────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 首頁          │                                                        │
│ 內容          │                                                        │
│  Albums       │   主區：PageHeader + 內容                               │
│  Photos       │                                                        │
│ 視圖          │                                                        │
│  相簿編排     │                                                        │
└───────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
帳號選單：顯示名、角色（「營運」「編輯」「管理員」「會員」，以「、」分隔）、登出。
```

相對 01 §7.2 B-S1 的修正：W1 沒有「⌘K 搜尋」與「媒體」區（§1.2）；「內容」在「視圖」之前（與 Home 的順序一致）。

| 項目 | 內容 |
| --- | --- |
| 資料來源 | `useSession().me`（W0）；`workQueries.types(api.work)` → `keys.types.list()` 提供 `pluralDisplayName` |
| 側欄規則 | `navFor(me, types)`：首頁（`end: true`）；「內容」＝`workTypes(me, types)`，順序＝`me.capabilities.types` 的順序（BW1a：依 key 字母序），標籤＝`pluralDisplayName`；「視圖」＝`viewsFor(me)`。沒有項目的區段不顯示 |
| loading | 類型清單還沒到：側欄只有「首頁」與「視圖」 |
| error | 類型清單失敗：同 loading（側欄不顯示錯誤；Home 顯示 `ErrorState`） |
| forbidden | 沒有 Back surface（member）：`SurfaceForbidden`，`h1`「沒有權限」、內文 `surface.forbidden.body`，不顯示 AppFrame |
| 未登入 | W0：導到 `/sign-in?returnTo=<目前路徑>` |

#### 5.0.2 Home（`/`，`HomePage`）

```
 作業台
 選擇要處理的內容或視圖。
 內容
 ┌───────────────┐ ┌───────────────┐ ┌───────────────┐
 │ Albums        │ │ Photos        │ │ …             │  每張卡片：pluralDisplayName（h3）、displayName
 │ Album         │ │ Photo         │ │               │
 └───────────────┘ └───────────────┘ └───────────────┘
 視圖
 ┌───────────────┐
 │ 相簿編排       │  每張卡片：視圖名稱（h3）、view.<key>.lead
 │ 調整相片順序…  │
 └───────────────┘
```

| 狀態 | 畫面 |
| --- | --- |
| loading | `DefaultSkeleton`（`query-loading`） |
| default | 兩個區段；「視圖」區段只在有視圖時出現 |
| empty | 沒有可作業的類型：`EmptyState`（`home-empty`），`home.noTypes`、`home.noTypesBody`，沒有按鈕 |
| error | `ErrorState`（`query-error`、`query-retry`） |

互動：卡片是連結（`home-type-<key>` → `/entries/<key>`；`home-view-<key>` → `/views/<key>`）。

#### 5.0.3 B-S2 Resource index（`/entries/:type`，`ResourceIndexPage` → `TypeGate` → `IndexBody`）

```
 Albums                                                     [新增Album]
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ [全部][草稿][已發布][已封存]                                           │ tab ↔ ?state=
│ [🔍 搜尋標題            ] [可見性：全部 ▾] [最近更新 ▾] [清除篩選]      │ ?q= ?filter.visibility= ?sort=
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 標題                 狀態                     可見性    排序方式  更新時間 │
│ Coast Light 2026     [已發布]                 [公開]    [手動]    2026年9月5日 上午10:00 │
│ Unlisted proof       [已發布][有未發布的變更] [不公開列出] [手動] …    │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 共 3 筆                          [上一頁] 第 1 / 1 頁 [下一頁] [20 ▾] │ ?page= ?size=
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

相對 01 §7.2 B-S2 的修正：「封面」欄不顯示（`media-ref` 的列表顯示在 W2 的 MediaPicker 之後統一處理；`FieldCell` 已支援，但 album 的 `cover` 欄 `listable: false`）；分頁用「上一頁／下一頁＋頁碼」而不是頁碼列（`total` 已知，頁碼列在 100 頁以上時太長）。

| 項目 | 內容 |
| --- | --- |
| 資料來源 | `TypeGate`：`workQueries.type(api.work, type)`；`IndexBody`：`workQueries.entries(api.work, type.key, toListParams(readIndexState(type, searchParams)))`，`placeholderData: keepPreviousData` |
| 欄位 | 第 1 欄「標題」＝連結，文字 `entry.title`，空字串或 `null` 時 `index.untitled`；「狀態」＝`StatusBadge`；再來是 `listable` 且不是 `titleField` 的欄位，依 `order` 排序，標題＝`fieldLabel`，內容＝`FieldCell`；最後「更新時間」＝`formatDateTime(updatedAt)` |
| 篩選 | `filterable`、`type === "enum"`、`enumValues` 非空的欄位，各一個 `Select`（選項：「<標籤>：全部」＋`enumLabel`） |
| 排序選項 | `index.sort.updatedDesc`（`-updatedAt`）、`updatedAsc`（`updatedAt`）、`titleAsc`（`title`）、`titleDesc`（`-title`）、`createdDesc`（`-createdAt`） |
| 主要動作 | `can(me, type, "create")` 且不是「已有條目的單例類型」：`新增<displayName>`（`entry-create`）→ `/entries/<type>/new`。單例判斷用目前查詢的 `total > 0` 且沒有縮小範圍（tab 全部、無搜尋、無篩選） |

| 狀態 | 畫面 |
| --- | --- |
| loading（第一次） | `IndexFilters` 可用；`IndexTable` 顯示 5 列 skeleton（`index-row-loading`），**不**顯示空狀態（C-02） |
| loading（換頁、換條件） | 舊資料留在畫面上直到新資料到達 |
| default | 表格（`index-table`、每列 `index-row`）＋`IndexPagination`（`index-pagination`） |
| empty（沒有縮小範圍） | `EmptyState`（`index-empty`）：`還沒有<pluralDisplayName>`；有 create 時說明 `index.emptyCreate`＋按鈕 `新增<displayName>`；沒有時說明 `index.emptyReadOnly`、無按鈕。不顯示分頁 |
| empty（有縮小範圍） | `EmptyState`（`index-no-match`）：`index.noMatch`、`index.noMatchBody`、按鈕「全部」（清掉所有參數）；篩選列另有「清除篩選」（`index-clear`） |
| error | `ErrorState`（`query-error`），重試按鈕重新查詢；不顯示分頁 |
| 頁碼超過最後一頁 | 讀到 `items` 為空、`total > 0`、`page > 1`：以 `replace` 導到最後一頁（W1-FM10） |
| forbidden | `TypeGate`：沒有 `read_draft`，或 API 回 403 → `/forbidden`（replace） |
| not-found | `TypeGate`：API 回 404 → `/not-found`（replace） |

| 互動 | 結果 |
| --- | --- |
| 點 tab | `?state=`（「全部」刪除參數）；`page` 刪除 |
| 搜尋框輸入 | 停止輸入 300ms 後寫入 `?q=`（`IndexFilters` 內建 debounce）；`page` 刪除 |
| 選篩選 | `?filter.<key>=`（「全部」刪除參數）；`page` 刪除 |
| 選排序 | `?sort=`（`-updatedAt` 刪除參數）；`page` 刪除 |
| 上一頁／下一頁 | `?page=`（1 刪除參數），其他參數保留 |
| 每頁筆數 | `?size=`（20 刪除參數）；`page` 刪除 |
| 點列的標題連結，或點列上的非連結、非按鈕處 | 開啟 `/entries/<type>/<id>` |

#### 5.0.4 B-S3 Resource details（`/entries/:type/:id`、`/entries/:type/new`）

```
 ← Albums
 Coast Light 2026  [已發布][有未發布的變更]              [⋯ 更多動作] [發布變更]
┌──────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┐

```

相對 01 §7.2 B-S3 的修正：沒有「預覽」按鈕與「修訂紀錄 →」（W2）；欄位分組改用 BW1a 的 `group`（G-05 已完成）；「封存／移到回收」在「⋯ 更多動作」選單。

| 項目 | 內容 |
| --- | --- |
| 資料來源 | `TypeGate`：`workQueries.type`；`EntryLoader`：`workQueries.entry(api.work, id)` → `keys.entries.detail(id)`；`RefValue`：`workQueries.entry`；`MediaValue`：`workQueries.media` → `keys.media.detail(id)` |
| 表單 | react-hook-form，`defaultValues = formOf(type, base)`＝`toFormValues(type, base.payload)` 加 `"$slug"`；resolver＝`saveResolver`（zod 的 save 規則＋「已設定的網址代稱不能清空」）；`mode: "onTouched"` |
| `base` | 編輯器自己保存「表單所依據的伺服器版本」。只在儲存、狀態轉換、載入最新版本成功後更新；背景 refetch **不**覆蓋使用者的輸入（V2-AC-09） |
| 分組 | `groupFields(type)`：主欄依序「基本資料 main、媒體 media、設定 settings、自訂群組（依第一次出現的順序）」，`titleField` 排在 main 第一個；`relations` 放側欄「關聯」卡 |
| 標題 | 既有：`base.title`，空時 `index.untitled`；新條目：`新增<displayName>` |
| 返回連結 | `← <pluralDisplayName>` → `/entries/<type>` |

**主要動作（§1.1 V2-AC-07）**，只會有一個：

| 條件 | 主要動作 |
| --- | --- |
| 新條目，表單不 dirty | 「建立」（`details-create`），送出表單 |
| 新條目，表單 dirty | 無（由儲存列的「建立」負責） |
| 既有，表單 dirty | 無 |
| 既有，不 dirty，有 `publish`，`draft` | 「發布」（`details-publish`） |
| 既有，不 dirty，有 `publish`，`published` 且 `base.dirty` | 「發布變更」（`details-publish`） |
| 其他（`published` 且不 dirty、`archived`、沒有 `publish`） | 無 |

**更多動作選單（`page-more-actions`）**，沒有任何項目時整個按鈕不出現：

| 條件 | 項目 | 行為 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 既有、有 `archive`、不是 `archived` | 封存（`details-archive`） | 開 `AlertDialog`（`confirm-archive`，標題 `details.archiveTitle`、內文 `details.archiveBody`、確認鈕 `confirm-archive-confirm`）→ `POST …/archive` |
| 既有、有 `archive`、`archived` | 還原為草稿（`details-restore`） | 直接 `POST …/restore` |
| 既有、有 `delete` | 移到回收（`details-delete`） | 開 `AlertDialog`（`confirm-delete`）→ `DELETE`；成功後以 `replace` 回到 `/entries/<type>` 並 toast `details.deleted` |

表單 dirty 或有請求進行中時，選單項目與「下架」一律 disabled（避免轉換狀態時把未存的輸入丟掉）。

| 狀態 | 畫面 |
| --- | --- |
| loading | `TypeGate` 或 `EntryLoader` 的 `DefaultSkeleton` |
| default | 上方線框 |
| 唯讀（`archived`） | `Alert`（`details-read-only`）`details.readOnlyArchived`；所有控件 disabled；沒有儲存列 |
| 唯讀（沒有 `update`） | `Alert` `details.readOnlyNoUpdate`；同上 |
| 儲存中 | 控件 disabled；儲存列按鈕顯示 `ui.save.saving` |
| 欄位錯誤 | 欄位下方 `field-<key>-error`（文字＝`fields.error.<FieldErrorCode>`）；網址代稱 `field-slug-error` |
| 衝突 | `Dialog`（`conflict-dialog`）：`conflict.title`、`conflict.body`、「繼續編輯」（`conflict-keep`）、「載入最新版本」（`conflict-reload`） |
| error（讀取失敗，非 403／404） | `ErrorState` |
| forbidden | 讀取 403 → `/forbidden`；`/new` 沒有 `create` → `/forbidden` |
| not-found | 讀取 404 → `/not-found` |
| 類型不符 | 條目的 `contentType` 與網址的 `:type` 不同 → `replace` 到 `/entries/<contentType>/<id>` |

| 互動 | 結果 |
| --- | --- |
| 修改任一欄 | 表單 dirty → 儲存列出現；主要動作消失 |
| 儲存（儲存列或 Enter 送出表單） | 先跑 resolver；有錯就標在欄位上、不送出。既有：`PATCH { version: base.version, payload: diffPayload(...), slug? }`，`slug` 只在與 `base.slug` 不同時送；新條目：`POST { slug: <值或 null>, payload: toPayload(...) }` |
| 儲存成功 | `setQueryData(detail)`、`invalidateQueries(lists(type))`、`base` 更新、表單 reset；toast `details.saved`（新條目 `details.created`，然後在表單不 dirty 後以 `replace` 導到 `/entries/<type>/<新 id>`） |
| 捨棄 | 表單 reset 回 `base` |
| 發布、發布變更 | 先以 `buildZod(type, "publish")` 檢查 `base` 的 payload，並檢查 `slugPolicy === "required"` 時 `base.slug` 非空；不通過就標欄位＋toast `details.publishBlocked`，不送出。通過才 `POST …/publish` |
| 狀態轉換成功 | 同儲存成功的快取處理；toast（`details.published`／`details.unpublished`／`details.archived`／`details.restored`） |
| 衝突 dialog「繼續編輯」 | 關閉；輸入保留，儲存列仍在；再次儲存仍會 409（版本沒變） |
| 衝突 dialog「載入最新版本」 | `fetchQuery(entry, staleTime: 0)` → `base` 更新、表單 reset 成伺服器內容 |
| 複製識別碼 | `navigator.clipboard.writeText(id)`；成功 toast `card.meta.copied` |
| 點側欄其他頁（dirty） | `leave-dialog`：「留在此頁」（`leave-stay`）或「捨棄並離開」（`leave-confirm`） |
| 關閉分頁或重新整理（dirty） | 瀏覽器的 `beforeunload` 提示 |

#### 5.0.5 `/forbidden`、`/not-found`、未知路徑

```
 沒有權限                                 找不到頁面
┌──────────────────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────────────────┐
│            ▢                 │         │            ▢                 │
│         沒有權限              │         │         找不到頁面            │
│ 你沒有檢視這個頁面的權限…      │         │ 這個頁面或項目不存在…          │
│         [回到首頁]            │         │         [回到首頁]            │
└──────────────────────────────┘         └──────────────────────────────┘
```

`EmptyState`（`forbidden`／`not-found`）＋`PageHeader`（h1）。在 AppFrame 內（已登入、有 Back surface）。未知路徑（`*`）顯示 `NotFoundPage`，網址不變。

#### 5.0.6 自訂視圖（只改文案與標籤）

| 視圖 | W1 的變化 | 資料來源 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `album.composer` | 「sortOrder 10」改為「第 N 張」（`composer.position`，N＝畫面上的位置）；通知改 toast；列表 `size: 100` | `workQueries.entries(api.work, "album", { size: 100 })`；photo 另加 `ref.album` |
| `clinic.schedule` | 時間用 `formatDateTime`；就診類型用 `enumLabel`；狀態用 `StatusBadge`；列表 `size: 100` | visit 列表＋`workQueries.type(api.work, "visit")` |
| `projects.board` | 欄位＝`issue.status` 的 `enumValues`（01 §13.2），欄名與選項＝`enumLabels`；說明改 `board.lead`；通知改 toast；列表 `size: 100` | project、issue 列表＋`workQueries.type(api.work, "issue")` |

行為（兩次 PATCH 交換順序、UTC 日期、`?project=` 只讀一次）維持 W0，W2 重做。視圖列表預設排序是 BW1b 的 `-updatedAt`：相簿與專案下拉選單的第一項因此是最近更新的那一筆（W0 是 fixture 順序）。

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import openapiTS, { astToString } from "openapi-typescript";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

// W1: codegen reads the latest back-end contract (01 Q-10: front and back meet in the integration stage).
const spec = new URL("../../../docs/v2/contracts/BW1c.openapi.yaml", import.meta.url);
const generated = new URL("./generated/schema.d.ts", import.meta.url);

describe("OpenAPI codegen", () => {
  it("E-02 src/generated/schema.d.ts is up to date with openapi.yaml (run `npm run gen -w @cms/api`)", async () => {
    const ast = await openapiTS(spec);
    const fresh = astToString(ast);
    const committed = readFileSync(fileURLToPath(generated), "utf8");
    expect(committed.endsWith(fresh)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`packages/api/src/errors.ts`（取代）：

```ts
import type { ErrorCode, FieldError } from "./schema";

/** Codes produced by the client itself, never by the server. */
export type ClientErrorCode = "NETWORK_ERROR" | "INVALID_RESPONSE";

export type ApiErrorCode = ErrorCode | ClientErrorCode;

/** The only error type thrown by @cms/api. `message` is a developer message, never shown to users. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: ApiErrorCode;
  readonly requestId: string | undefined;
  /** Every invalid payload key on a 422 (BW1c `error.fields`); empty otherwise. */
  readonly fields: FieldError[];

  constructor(status: number, code: ApiErrorCode, message: string, requestId?: string, fields: FieldError[] = []) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.requestId = requestId;
    this.fields = fields;
  }
}

export function isApiError(value: unknown): value is ApiError {
  return value instanceof ApiError;
}

/** Turns an error response body into ApiError. Bodies that are not an ErrorEnvelope become INVALID_RESPONSE (C-18). */
export function errorFromBody(status: number, body: unknown): ApiError {
  if (body && typeof body === "object" && "error" in body) {
    const envelope = body as { error?: { code?: unknown; message?: unknown; fields?: unknown }; requestId?: unknown };
    const code = envelope.error?.code;
    if (typeof code === "string") {
      const message = typeof envelope.error?.message === "string" ? envelope.error.message : "";
      const requestId = typeof envelope.requestId === "string" ? envelope.requestId : undefined;
      const fields = Array.isArray(envelope.error?.fields) ? (envelope.error.fields as FieldError[]) : [];
      return new ApiError(status, code as ErrorCode, message, requestId, fields);
    }
  }
  return new ApiError(status, "INVALID_RESPONSE", `HTTP ${status} without an error envelope`);
}
```

```ts
// Query key factory (01 §4.3). Every useQuery and invalidateQueries uses these; never write a key literal.
export interface PublicListParams {
  q?: string;
  /** ref.<fieldKey>=<entry id> */
  ref?: Record<string, string>;
  /** filter.<fieldKey>=<value>; datetime ranges use the keys "<fieldKey>.from" and "<fieldKey>.to" (BW1b §4.3). */
  filter?: Record<string, string>;
  /** 1-based; the API default is 1. */
  page?: number;
  /** 1–100; the API default is 20. */
  size?: number;
  /** One sort key, "-" prefix for descending, for example "-updatedAt". */
  sort?: string;
}

export interface WorkListParams extends PublicListParams {
  /** Comma-separated publication states; omitted means draft,published (BW1b). */
  state?: string;
}

export const keys = {
  auth: {
    me: () => ["auth", "me"] as const,
    /** true after a 401 while signed in (session expired); cleared by login and sign-out. */
    expired: () => ["auth", "expired"] as const,
  },
  public: {
    all: () => ["public"] as const,
    entries: (type: string, params: PublicListParams = {}) => ["public", "entries", type, params] as const,
    bySlug: (type: string, slug: string) => ["public", "bySlug", type, slug] as const,
    byId: (type: string, id: string) => ["public", "byId", type, id] as const,
  },
  types: {
    all: () => ["types"] as const,
    list: () => ["types", "list"] as const,
    detail: (type: string) => ["types", "detail", type] as const,
  },
  entries: {
    all: () => ["entries"] as const,
    lists: (type: string) => ["entries", "list", type] as const,
    list: (type: string, params: WorkListParams = {}) => ["entries", "list", type, params] as const,
    detail: (id: string) => ["entries", "detail", id] as const,
  },
  media: {
    detail: (id: string) => ["media", "detail", id] as const,
  },
  admin: {
    types: () => ["admin", "types"] as const,
    principals: () => ["admin", "principals"] as const,
  },
};
```

`packages/api/src/query.ts`（取代）：

```ts
import type { PublicListParams } from "./keys";

// `ref.<fieldKey>` and `filter.<fieldKey>` are documented in the operation descriptions, but OpenAPI 3.0 cannot
// name a parameter pattern, so they are absent from the generated query type. This is the only place that adds them.
export function listQuery<Q extends object>(typed: Q, params: PublicListParams): Q {
  const query: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(typed)) {
    if (typeof value === "string" && value !== "") query[key] = value;
    if (typeof value === "number") query[key] = String(value);
  }
  for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(params.filter ?? {})) {
    if (value !== "") query[`filter.${field}`] = value;
  }
  for (const [field, id] of Object.entries(params.ref ?? {})) {
    if (id !== "") query[`ref.${field}`] = id;
  }
  return query as Q;
}
```

`packages/api/src/work.ts`（取代）：

```ts
import { queryOptions } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { Transport } from "./core";
import { keys, type WorkListParams } from "./keys";
import { listQuery } from "./query";
import type { EntryPatchRequest, EntryWriteRequest, MediaAsset, WorkContentType, WorkContentTypeList, WorkEntry, WorkEntryPage } from "./schema";

export function workApi(t: Transport) {
  return {
    types(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorkContentTypeList> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.GET("/api/v1/content-types", { signal }));
    },
    type(type: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorkContentType> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.GET("/api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}", { params: { path: { typeKey: type } }, signal }));
    },
    entries(type: string, params: WorkListParams = {}, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorkEntryPage> {
      return t.call(() =>
        t.client.GET("/api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}/entries", {
          params: { path: { typeKey: type }, query: listQuery({ q: params.q, state: params.state, page: params.page, size: params.size, sort: params.sort }, params) },
          signal,
        }),
      );
    },
    entry(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.GET("/api/v1/entries/{id}", { params: { path: { id } }, signal }));
    },
    create(type: string, body: EntryWriteRequest): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.POST("/api/v1/content-types/{typeKey}/entries", { params: { path: { typeKey: type } }, body }));
    },
    /** `version` is required (BW1c: a missing version is 428 VERSION_REQUIRED). */
    patch(id: string, body: EntryPatchRequest): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.PATCH("/api/v1/entries/{id}", { params: { path: { id } }, body }));
    },
    publish(id: string): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.POST("/api/v1/entries/{id}/publish", { params: { path: { id } } }));
    },
    unpublish(id: string): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.POST("/api/v1/entries/{id}/unpublish", { params: { path: { id } } }));
    },
    archive(id: string): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.POST("/api/v1/entries/{id}/archive", { params: { path: { id } } }));
    },
    restore(id: string): Promise<WorkEntry> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.POST("/api/v1/entries/{id}/restore", { params: { path: { id } } }));
    },
    /** Soft delete ("移到回收"). */
    remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.DELETE("/api/v1/entries/{id}", { params: { path: { id } } }));
    },
    media(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<MediaAsset> {
      return t.call(() => t.client.GET("/api/v1/media/{id}", { params: { path: { id } }, signal }));
    },
    upload(file: File, title?: string): Promise<MediaAsset> {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      if (title) form.append("title", title);
      return t.call(() =>
        t.client.POST("/api/v1/media", {
          // MediaUploadRequest is multipart; openapi-fetch sends a FormData body unchanged.
          body: form as unknown as { file: string },
          bodySerializer: (body) => body as unknown as FormData,
        }),
      );
    },
  };
}

export type WorkApi = ReturnType<typeof workApi>;

export const workQueries = {
  types: (api: WorkApi) => queryOptions({ queryKey: keys.types.list(), queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.types(signal) }),
  type: (api: WorkApi, type: string) =>
    queryOptions({ queryKey: keys.types.detail(type), queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.type(type, signal) }),
  entries: (api: WorkApi, type: string, params: WorkListParams = {}) =>
    queryOptions({ queryKey: keys.entries.list(type, params), queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.entries(type, params, signal) }),
  entry: (api: WorkApi, id: string) =>
    queryOptions({ queryKey: keys.entries.detail(id), queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.entry(id, signal) }),
  media: (api: WorkApi, id: string) =>
    queryOptions({ queryKey: keys.media.detail(id), queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.media(id, signal) }),
};
```

#### 5.2.1 元件規格

| 元件 | 檔案 | props | 內部狀態 | 狀態與畫面 | `data-testid` | copy key |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `StatusBadge` | `patterns/status-badge.tsx` | `{ state: "draft" \| "published" \| "archived"; dirty?: boolean }` | 無 | draft：`bg-surface-subdued`；published：`bg-success-bg text-success`；archived：`text-subdued`；published＋dirty：再加 `bg-caution-bg text-caution` 的第二個 badge | `status-badge`（`data-state`＝狀態） | `ui.status.draft`、`published`、`archived`、`dirty` |
| `ResourceLayout` | `patterns/resource-layout.tsx` | `{ main: ReactNode; aside: ReactNode }` | 無 | ≥ 1024px：2fr＋1fr；以下：單欄，aside 在後 | `resource-layout` | — |
| `IndexTable<T>` | `patterns/index-table.tsx` | `IndexTableProps<T>`（`columns`、`rows`、`rowKey`、`rowHref`、`rowLabel`、`loading`、`empty`） | 無 | loading 或 `rows` undefined：5 列 skeleton；載入完成且 0 列：`empty`；否則表格。第一欄一律是 `rowHref` 的連結 | `index-table`、`index-row`、`index-row-loading` | — |
| `IndexFilters` | `patterns/index-filters.tsx` | `IndexFiltersProps`（tab、搜尋、篩選、排序、`onClear?`） | `text`（搜尋框的即時值；300ms 後回呼） | `onClear` 有值時顯示「清除篩選」 | `index-filters`、`tab-<value>`、`index-search`、`filter-<key>`、`index-sort`、`index-clear` | `ui.index.search`、`sort`、`clear`、`filterAll` |
| `IndexPagination` | `patterns/index-pagination.tsx` | `{ page; size; total; onPageChange; onSizeChange }` | 無 | 第一頁時「上一頁」disabled；最後一頁時「下一頁」disabled | `index-pagination`、`index-total`、`page-previous`、`page-current`、`page-next`、`page-size` | `ui.pagination.*` |
| `ContextualSaveBar` | `patterns/contextual-save-bar.tsx` | `{ dirty; saving; onSave; onDiscard; saveLabel? }` | 無（`useBlocker`） | dirty：頂部固定 `bg-primary` 列；saving：按鈕 disabled、文字 `ui.save.saving`；路由離開被擋時 `AlertDialog` | `save-bar`、`save-bar-save`、`save-bar-discard`、`leave-dialog`、`leave-stay`、`leave-confirm` | `ui.save.*`、`ui.leave.*` |
| `Toaster`、`toast` | `patterns/toaster.tsx` | 無 | sonner 內部 | `position="top-center"`；成功 `toast.success`、失敗 `toast.error` | sonner 的 `[data-sonner-toast]` | 呼叫端的 copy |
| `PageHeader`（修改） | `patterns/page-header.tsx` | W0 的 props 加 `moreActions?: PageAction[]` | 無 | 選單＝（次要動作 > 2 時的次要動作）＋`moreActions`；選單為空時不顯示按鈕。所有按鈕 `type="button"`（W1-FM08） | `page-header`、`page-more-actions`、各 action 的 `testId` | `ui.actions.more` |

```ts
  "ui.leave.leave": "捨棄並離開",
} as const;

export type UiCopyKey = keyof typeof uiCopy;

/** Replaces `{name}` placeholders in a copy string. The only formatting helper; used for counts and page numbers. */
export function fill(text: string, values: Record<string, string | number>): string {
  return text.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, name: string) => String(values[name] ?? ""));
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/status-badge.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import { Badge } from "../components/ui/badge";
import { cn } from "../lib/utils";
import { uiCopy } from "../copy";

export type PublicationStateValue = "draft" | "published" | "archived";

const TONE: Record<PublicationStateValue, string> = {
  draft: "bg-surface-subdued text-foreground border-border",
  published: "bg-success-bg text-success border-transparent",
  archived: "bg-surface-subdued text-subdued border-border",
};

/** 01 §6.2: the publication state as a badge, plus "有未發布的變更" when a published entry is dirty. */
export function StatusBadge({ state, dirty = false }: { state: PublicationStateValue; dirty?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex flex-wrap gap-1" data-testid="status-badge" data-state={state}>
      <Badge variant="outline" className={cn("rounded-full", TONE[state])}>
        {uiCopy[`ui.status.${state}`]}
      </Badge>
      {state === "published" && dirty ? (
        <Badge variant="outline" className="rounded-full border-transparent bg-caution-bg text-caution">
          {uiCopy["ui.status.dirty"]}
        </Badge>
      ) : null}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/index-table.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { Skeleton } from "../components/ui/skeleton";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "../components/ui/table";

export interface IndexColumn<T> {
  key: string;
  header: string;
  cell: (row: T) => ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export interface IndexTableProps<T> {
  columns: IndexColumn<T>[];
  rows: T[] | undefined;
  rowKey: (row: T) => string;
  /** Link target of a row. The first column renders it as a real <a>; clicking elsewhere in the row navigates too. */
  rowHref: (row: T) => string;
  /** Title of the first column's link, for example the entry title. */
  rowLabel: (row: T) => ReactNode;
  loading: boolean;
  /** Shown instead of the table when loaded with zero rows. */
  empty: ReactNode;
}

/** 01 §6.2: a table whose rows open a resource. Loading shows skeleton rows; never the empty slot (C-02). */
export function IndexTable<T>({ columns, rows, rowKey, rowHref, rowLabel, loading, empty }: IndexTableProps<T>) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  if (!loading && rows && rows.length === 0) return <>{empty}</>;
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-surface" data-testid="index-table">
      <Table className="text-table">
        <TableHeader className="bg-surface-subdued">
          <TableRow>
            {columns.map((column) => (
              <TableHead key={column.key} className={column.className}>
                {column.header}
              </TableHead>
            ))}
          </TableRow>
        </TableHeader>
        <TableBody>
          {loading || !rows
            ? Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => (
                <TableRow key={i} data-testid="index-row-loading">
                  {columns.map((column) => (
                    <TableCell key={column.key}>
                      <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
                    </TableCell>
                  ))}
                </TableRow>
              ))
            : rows.map((row) => (
                <TableRow
                  key={rowKey(row)}
                  className="cursor-pointer"
                  data-testid="index-row"
                  onClick={(event) => {
                    if ((event.target as HTMLElement).closest("a,button,input,select")) return;
                    navigate(rowHref(row));
                  }}
                >
                  {columns.map((column, index) => (
                    <TableCell key={column.key} className={column.className}>
                      {index === 0 ? (
                        <Link to={rowHref(row)} className="font-semibold hover:underline">
                          {rowLabel(row)}
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        column.cell(row)
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                  ))}
                </TableRow>
              ))}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/index-pagination.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import { Button } from "../components/ui/button";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "../components/ui/select";
import { fill, uiCopy } from "../copy";

export const PAGE_SIZES = [20, 50, 100] as const;

export interface IndexPaginationProps {
  page: number;
  size: number;
  total: number;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
  onSizeChange: (size: number) => void;
}

/** "共 N 筆", previous / next and page size (surface-back §3.5: page from 1, size up to 100). */
export function IndexPagination({ page, size, total, onPageChange, onSizeChange }: IndexPaginationProps) {
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / size));
  return (
    <nav aria-label={uiCopy["ui.pagination.size"]} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-3 rounded-b-xl border border-t-0 bg-surface p-3 text-table" data-testid="index-pagination">
      <span className="mr-auto text-subdued" data-testid="index-total">
        {fill(uiCopy["ui.pagination.total"], { total })}
      </span>
      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => onPageChange(page - 1)} data-testid="page-previous">
        {uiCopy["ui.pagination.previous"]}
      </Button>
      <span data-testid="page-current">{fill(uiCopy["ui.pagination.page"], { page, pages })}</span>
      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)} data-testid="page-next">
        {uiCopy["ui.pagination.next"]}
      </Button>
      <Select value={String(size)} onValueChange={(value) => onSizeChange(Number(value))}>
        <SelectTrigger aria-label={uiCopy["ui.pagination.size"]} size="sm" className="w-24" data-testid="page-size">
          <SelectValue />
        </SelectTrigger>
        <SelectContent>
          {PAGE_SIZES.map((option) => (
            <SelectItem key={option} value={String(option)}>
              {option}
            </SelectItem>
          ))}
        </SelectContent>
      </Select>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/index-filters.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { SearchIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "../components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "../components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "../components/ui/select";
import { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "../components/ui/tabs";
import { fill, uiCopy } from "../copy";

export interface FilterOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

export interface IndexFilter {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  options: FilterOption[];
  /** "" means no filter. */
  value: string;
}

export interface IndexFiltersProps {
  tabs: FilterOption[];
  tab: string;
  onTabChange: (value: string) => void;
  query: string;
  /** Called 300ms after the user stops typing (01 §6.2). */
  onQueryChange: (value: string) => void;
  filters: IndexFilter[];
  onFilterChange: (key: string, value: string) => void;
  sortOptions: FilterOption[];
  sort: string;
  onSortChange: (value: string) => void;
  /** Shown when any filter or query is set. */
  onClear?: () => void;
}

const ANY = "__any__";

/** 01 §6.2: state tabs, debounced search, enum filters and sort; the page keeps all of them in the URL. */
export function IndexFilters(props: IndexFiltersProps) {
  const { tabs, tab, onTabChange, query, onQueryChange, filters, onFilterChange, sortOptions, sort, onSortChange, onClear } = props;
  const [text, setText] = useState(query);
  useEffect(() => setText(query), [query]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (text === query) return undefined;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => onQueryChange(text), 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [text, query, onQueryChange]);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-t-xl border border-b-0 bg-surface p-3" data-testid="index-filters">
      <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={onTabChange}>
        <TabsList>
          {tabs.map((option) => (
            // No TabsContent: the tabs filter the table below, so the generated aria-controls would point nowhere (axe).
            <TabsTrigger key={option.value} value={option.value} aria-controls={undefined} data-testid={`tab-${option.value}`}>
              {option.label}
            </TabsTrigger>
          ))}
        </TabsList>
      </Tabs>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <div className="relative min-w-48 flex-1">
          <SearchIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 size-4 text-subdued" aria-hidden="true" />
          <Input
            aria-label={uiCopy["ui.index.search"]}
            placeholder={uiCopy["ui.index.search"]}
            className="pl-8"
            value={text}
            onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
            data-testid="index-search"
          />
        </div>
        {filters.map((filter) => (
          <Select key={filter.key} value={filter.value || ANY} onValueChange={(value) => onFilterChange(filter.key, value === ANY ? "" : value)}>
            <SelectTrigger aria-label={filter.label} className="w-40" data-testid={`filter-${filter.key}`}>
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value={ANY}>{fill(uiCopy["ui.index.filterAll"], { label: filter.label })}</SelectItem>
              {filter.options.map((option) => (
                <SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                  {option.label}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        ))}
        <Select value={sort} onValueChange={onSortChange}>
          <SelectTrigger aria-label={uiCopy["ui.index.sort"]} className="w-40" data-testid="index-sort">
            <SelectValue />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            {sortOptions.map((option) => (
              <SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                {option.label}
              </SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
        {onClear ? (
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClear} data-testid="index-clear">
            {uiCopy["ui.index.clear"]}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/contextual-save-bar.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useBlocker } from "react-router";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "../components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Button } from "../components/ui/button";
import { uiCopy } from "../copy";

export interface ContextualSaveBarProps {
  dirty: boolean;
  saving: boolean;
  onSave: () => void;
  onDiscard: () => void;
  /** Label of the save button; defaults to "儲存". */
  saveLabel?: string;
}

/**
 * 01 §6.2: while dirty, a bar over the top bar offers 捨棄 / 儲存; leaving by the router asks for confirmation and
 * closing or reloading the tab triggers the browser's own prompt (beforeunload). Fixes C-06. Its buttons are
 * type="button", so a bar rendered inside a <form> saves once, through onSave.
 */
export function ContextualSaveBar({ dirty, saving, onSave, onDiscard, saveLabel }: ContextualSaveBarProps) {
  const blocker = useBlocker(({ currentLocation, nextLocation }) => dirty && currentLocation.pathname !== nextLocation.pathname);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) return undefined;
    const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.returnValue = "";
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
  }, [dirty]);
  return (
    <>
      {dirty ? (
        <div role="region" aria-label={uiCopy["ui.save.title"]} className="fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-50 flex h-14 items-center gap-2 bg-primary px-4 text-primary-foreground lg:px-6" data-testid="save-bar">
          <span className="mr-auto text-card-title">{uiCopy["ui.save.title"]}</span>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="bg-transparent text-primary-foreground hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-primary-foreground" onClick={onDiscard} disabled={saving} data-testid="save-bar-discard">
            {uiCopy["ui.save.discard"]}
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={onSave} disabled={saving} data-testid="save-bar-save">
            {saving ? uiCopy["ui.save.saving"] : (saveLabel ?? uiCopy["ui.save.save"])}
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <AlertDialog open={blocker.state === "blocked"}>
        <AlertDialogContent data-testid="leave-dialog">
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>{uiCopy["ui.leave.title"]}</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>{uiCopy["ui.leave.body"]}</AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel onClick={() => blocker.reset?.()} data-testid="leave-stay">
              {uiCopy["ui.leave.stay"]}
            </AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => blocker.proceed?.()} data-testid="leave-confirm">
              {uiCopy["ui.leave.leave"]}
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/page-header.tsx`（取代）：

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { ArrowLeftIcon, EllipsisIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "../components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "../components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { uiCopy } from "../copy";
import { useDocumentTitle } from "./document-title";

export interface PageAction {
  label: string;
  /** Router path; renders a link. */
  to?: string;
  onSelect?: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  testId?: string;
}

export interface PageHeaderProps {
  title: string;
  backTo?: { to: string; label: string };
  badges?: ReactNode;
  /** More than two are collapsed into the "更多動作" menu (01 §6.2). */
  secondaryActions?: PageAction[];
  /** Always listed in the "更多動作" menu, after any collapsed secondary actions (01 §7.2: 封存, 移到回收). */
  moreActions?: PageAction[];
  primaryAction?: PageAction;
}

function ActionButton({ action, variant }: { action: PageAction; variant: "default" | "outline" }) {
  if (action.to) {
    return (
      <Button asChild variant={variant} data-testid={action.testId}>
        <Link to={action.to}>{action.label}</Link>
      </Button>
    );
  }
  return (
    // type="button": a details page renders the header inside its <form>, and an action must never submit it.
    <Button type="button" variant={variant} onClick={action.onSelect} disabled={action.disabled} data-testid={action.testId}>
      {action.label}
    </Button>
  );
}

export function PageHeader({ title, backTo, badges, secondaryActions = [], moreActions = [], primaryAction }: PageHeaderProps) {
  useDocumentTitle(title);
  const collapse = secondaryActions.length > 2;
  const menu = [...(collapse ? secondaryActions : []), ...moreActions];
  return (
    <header className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3" data-testid="page-header">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1">
        {backTo ? (
          <Link to={backTo.to} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-subdued hover:underline">
            <ArrowLeftIcon className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            {backTo.label}
          </Link>
        ) : null}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-page-title">{title}</h1>
          {badges}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {collapse ? null : secondaryActions.map((action) => <ActionButton key={action.label} action={action} variant="outline" />)}
        {menu.length > 0 ? (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" data-testid="page-more-actions">
                <EllipsisIcon aria-hidden="true" />
                {uiCopy["ui.actions.more"]}
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
              {menu.map((action) => (
                <DropdownMenuItem
                  key={action.label}
                  disabled={action.disabled}
                  onSelect={action.onSelect}
                  data-testid={action.testId}
                  asChild={Boolean(action.to)}
                >
                  {action.to ? <Link to={action.to}>{action.label}</Link> : action.label}
                </DropdownMenuItem>
              ))}
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        ) : null}
        {primaryAction ? <ActionButton action={primaryAction} variant="default" /> : null}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/patterns/patterns-w1.test.tsx`（新增）：

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { createMemoryRouter, Link, MemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from "react-router";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ContextualSaveBar } from "./contextual-save-bar";
import { IndexFilters } from "./index-filters";
import { IndexPagination } from "./index-pagination";
import { IndexTable } from "./index-table";
import { PageHeader } from "./page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "./status-badge";

type Row = { id: string; title: string };
const columns = [
  { key: "title", header: "標題", cell: (r: Row) => r.title },
  { key: "id", header: "代號", cell: (r: Row) => r.id },
];

function table(rows: Row[] | undefined, loading: boolean) {
  return (
    <MemoryRouter>
      <IndexTable columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.id} rowHref={(r) => `/x/${r.id}`} rowLabel={(r) => r.title} loading={loading} empty={<p>空</p>} />
    </MemoryRouter>
  );
}

describe("W1 patterns", () => {
  it("C-06 StatusBadge shows the state and, for a dirty published entry, the change badge", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<StatusBadge state="draft" dirty />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("status-badge")).toHaveTextContent("草稿");
    expect(screen.queryByText("有未發布的變更")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    rerender(<StatusBadge state="published" dirty />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("status-badge")).toHaveTextContent("已發布有未發布的變更");
  });

  it("C-02 IndexTable shows skeleton rows while loading and the empty slot only when loaded empty", () => {
    const { rerender } = render(table(undefined, true));
    expect(screen.getAllByTestId("index-row-loading")).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(screen.queryByText("空")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    rerender(table([], false));
    expect(screen.getByText("空")).toBeInTheDocument();
    rerender(table([{ id: "a", title: "甲" }], false));
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "甲" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/x/a");
  });

  it("U-04 IndexFilters calls onQueryChange 300ms after typing stops", () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const onQueryChange = vi.fn();
    render(
      <IndexFilters
        tabs={[{ value: "all", label: "全部" }]}
        tab="all"
        onTabChange={vi.fn()}
        query=""
        onQueryChange={onQueryChange}
        filters={[]}
        onFilterChange={vi.fn()}
        sortOptions={[{ value: "-updatedAt", label: "最近更新" }]}
        sort="-updatedAt"
        onSortChange={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByTestId("index-search"), { target: { value: "co" } });
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(299));
    expect(onQueryChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1));
    expect(onQueryChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith("co");
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it("G-02 IndexPagination shows the total and disables the ends", () => {
    const onPageChange = vi.fn();
    render(<IndexPagination page={1} size={20} total={45} onPageChange={onPageChange} onSizeChange={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("index-total")).toHaveTextContent("共 45 筆");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("page-current")).toHaveTextContent("第 1 / 3 頁");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("page-previous")).toBeDisabled();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("page-next"));
    expect(onPageChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
  });

  it("V2-AC-08 ContextualSaveBar blocks in-app navigation while dirty and saves or discards on request", async () => {
    const onSave = vi.fn();
    const onDiscard = vi.fn();
    const router = createMemoryRouter(
      [
        {
          path: "/edit",
          element: (
            <>
              <ContextualSaveBar dirty saving={false} onSave={onSave} onDiscard={onDiscard} />
              <Link to="/other">其他頁</Link>
            </>
          ),
        },
        { path: "/other", element: <p>其他</p> },
      ],
      { initialEntries: ["/edit"] },
    );
    render(<RouterProvider router={router} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("save-bar-save"));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("save-bar-discard"));
    expect(onSave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onDiscard).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "其他頁" }));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("leave-dialog")).toBeInTheDocument();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("leave-stay"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/edit"));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "其他頁" }));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByTestId("leave-confirm"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/other"));
  });

  it("V2-AC-08 ContextualSaveBar registers beforeunload only while dirty", () => {
    const add = vi.spyOn(window, "addEventListener");
    const router = createMemoryRouter([{ path: "/", element: <ContextualSaveBar dirty={false} saving={false} onSave={vi.fn()} onDiscard={vi.fn()} /> }]);
    render(<RouterProvider router={router} />);
    expect(add.mock.calls.some(([type]) => type === "beforeunload")).toBe(false);
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("save-bar")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    add.mockRestore();
  });

  it("W1-FM08 inside a <form>, the save bar and header actions never submit the form", () => {
    const onSubmit = vi.fn((event: { preventDefault: () => void }) => event.preventDefault());
    const onSave = vi.fn();
    const onAction = vi.fn();
    const router = createMemoryRouter([
      {
        path: "/",
        element: (
          <form onSubmit={onSubmit}>
            <PageHeader title="甲" primaryAction={{ label: "發布", onSelect: onAction }} />
            <ContextualSaveBar dirty saving={false} onSave={onSave} onDiscard={() => {}} />
          </form>
        ),
      },
    ]);
    render(<RouterProvider router={router} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("save-bar-save"));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "發布" }));
    expect(onSave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onAction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"
import { Tabs as TabsPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

function Tabs({
  className,
  orientation = "horizontal",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof TabsPrimitive.Root>) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Root
      data-slot="tabs"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(
        "group/tabs flex gap-2 data-[orientation=horizontal]:flex-col",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const tabsListVariants = cva(
  "group/tabs-list inline-flex w-fit items-center justify-center rounded-lg p-[3px] text-muted-foreground group-data-[orientation=horizontal]/tabs:h-9 group-data-[orientation=vertical]/tabs:h-fit group-data-[orientation=vertical]/tabs:flex-col data-[variant=line]:rounded-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-muted",
        line: "gap-1 bg-transparent",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function TabsList({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof TabsPrimitive.List> &

        "group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:bg-transparent group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-[state=active]:bg-transparent dark:group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-[state=active]:border-transparent dark:group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-[state=active]:bg-transparent",

        "after:absolute after:bg-foreground after:opacity-0 after:transition-opacity group-data-[orientation=horizontal]/tabs:after:inset-x-0 group-data-[orientation=horizontal]/tabs:after:bottom-[-5px] group-data-[orientation=horizontal]/tabs:after:h-0.5 group-data-[orientation=vertical]/tabs:after:inset-y-0 group-data-[orientation=vertical]/tabs:after:-right-1 group-data-[orientation=vertical]/tabs:after:w-0.5 group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-[state=active]:after:opacity-100",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />

}

function TabsContent({

}
```

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"
import { CheckIcon, ChevronDownIcon, ChevronUpIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { Select as SelectPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

function Select({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <SelectPrimitive.Root data-slot="select" {...props} />
}

function SelectGroup({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Group>) {
  return <SelectPrimitive.Group data-slot="select-group" {...props} />
}

function SelectValue({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Value>) {
  return <SelectPrimitive.Value data-slot="select-value" {...props} />
}

function SelectTrigger({
  className,
  size = "default",
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Trigger> & {
  size?: "sm" | "default"
}) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.Trigger
      data-slot="select-trigger"
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(
        "flex w-fit items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-sm whitespace-nowrap shadow-xs transition-[color,box-shadow] outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 data-[placeholder]:text-muted-foreground data-[size=default]:h-9 data-[size=sm]:h-8 *:data-[slot=select-value]:line-clamp-1 *:data-[slot=select-value]:flex *:data-[slot=select-value]:items-center *:data-[slot=select-value]:gap-2 dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg:not([class*='text-'])]:text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <SelectPrimitive.Icon asChild>
        <ChevronDownIcon className="size-4 opacity-50" />
      </SelectPrimitive.Icon>
    </SelectPrimitive.Trigger>
  )
}

function SelectContent({
  className,
  children,
  position = "item-aligned",
  align = "center",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Content>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.Portal>
      <SelectPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="select-content"
        className={cn(
          "relative z-50 max-h-(--radix-select-content-available-height) min-w-[8rem] origin-(--radix-select-content-transform-origin) overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto rounded-md border bg-popover text-popover-foreground shadow-md data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-2 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-2 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95",
          position === "popper" &&
            "data-[side=bottom]:translate-y-1 data-[side=left]:-translate-x-1 data-[side=right]:translate-x-1 data-[side=top]:-translate-y-1",
          className
        )}
        position={position}
        align={align}
        {...props}
      >
        <SelectScrollUpButton />
        <SelectPrimitive.Viewport
          className={cn(
            "p-1",
            position === "popper" &&
              "h-[var(--radix-select-trigger-height)] w-full min-w-[var(--radix-select-trigger-width)] scroll-my-1"
          )}
        >
          {children}
        </SelectPrimitive.Viewport>
        <SelectScrollDownButton />
      </SelectPrimitive.Content>
    </SelectPrimitive.Portal>
  )
}

function SelectLabel({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Label>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.Label
      data-slot="select-label"
      className={cn("px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SelectItem({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Item>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.Item
      data-slot="select-item"
      className={cn(
        "relative flex w-full cursor-default items-center gap-2 rounded-sm py-1.5 pr-8 pl-2 text-sm outline-hidden select-none focus:bg-accent focus:text-accent-foreground data-[disabled]:pointer-events-none data-[disabled]:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg:not([class*='text-'])]:text-muted-foreground *:[span]:last:flex *:[span]:last:items-center *:[span]:last:gap-2",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <span
        data-slot="select-item-indicator"
        className="absolute right-2 flex size-3.5 items-center justify-center"
      >
        <SelectPrimitive.ItemIndicator>
          <CheckIcon className="size-4" />
        </SelectPrimitive.ItemIndicator>
      </span>
      <SelectPrimitive.ItemText>{children}</SelectPrimitive.ItemText>
    </SelectPrimitive.Item>
  )
}

function SelectSeparator({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.Separator>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.Separator
      data-slot="select-separator"
      className={cn("pointer-events-none -mx-1 my-1 h-px bg-border", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SelectScrollUpButton({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.ScrollUpButton>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.ScrollUpButton
      data-slot="select-scroll-up-button"
      className={cn(
        "flex cursor-default items-center justify-center py-1",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <ChevronUpIcon className="size-4" />
    </SelectPrimitive.ScrollUpButton>
  )
}

function SelectScrollDownButton({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SelectPrimitive.ScrollDownButton>) {
  return (
    <SelectPrimitive.ScrollDownButton
      data-slot="select-scroll-down-button"
      className={cn(
        "flex cursor-default items-center justify-center py-1",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <ChevronDownIcon className="size-4" />
    </SelectPrimitive.ScrollDownButton>
  )
}

export {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectGroup,
  SelectItem,
  SelectLabel,
  SelectScrollDownButton,
  SelectScrollUpButton,
  SelectSeparator,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
}
```

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

import { Button } from "./button"

function Dialog({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="dialog" {...props} />
}

function DialogTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dialog-trigger" {...props} />
}

function DialogPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dialog-portal" {...props} />
}

function DialogClose({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Close>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" {...props} />
}

function DialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogContent({
  className,
  children,
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPortal data-slot="dialog-portal">
      <DialogOverlay />
      <DialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="dialog-content"
        className={cn(
          "fixed top-[50%] left-[50%] z-50 grid w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] gap-4 rounded-lg border bg-background p-6 shadow-lg duration-200 outline-none data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 sm:max-w-lg",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <DialogPrimitive.Close
            data-slot="dialog-close"
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 rounded-xs opacity-70 ring-offset-background transition-opacity hover:opacity-100 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2 focus:outline-hidden disabled:pointer-events-none data-[state=open]:bg-accent data-[state=open]:text-muted-foreground [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4"
          >
            <XIcon />
            <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
          </DialogPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    </DialogPortal>
  )
}

function DialogHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2 text-center sm:text-left", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogFooter({
  className,
  showCloseButton = false,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-footer"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      {showCloseButton && (
        <DialogPrimitive.Close asChild>
          <Button variant="outline">Close</Button>
        </DialogPrimitive.Close>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function DialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="dialog-title"
      className={cn("text-lg leading-none font-semibold", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="dialog-description"
      className={cn("text-sm text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogOverlay,
  DialogPortal,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
}
```
